[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:6:2)


**והמה בוכים.** מתפללים לשם:


WHILE THEY WERE WEEPING. Praying to God.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:7:1)


**וירא פינחס.** כאשר ראה פינחס כן קם מתוך העדה שהיתה בחצר אהל מועד ויש בכאן שאלה ויתכן להשיב שכבר נצמד זמרי בעדים:


AND WHEN PHINEHAS…SAW. When Phinehas saw this, he rose up from the midst of the congregation, which was in the courtyard of the tent of meeting. There is a question here.¹¹ (Weiser). It is possible to answer that Zimri (v. 14) was joined¹². in the presence of witnesses.

footnotes:
¹¹ Why did Phinehas kill Zimri? Scripture does not tell us that Zimri worshipped pe’or
¹² With Cozbi in the worship of pe’or



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:1)


**אל הקבה.** כמו אל האהל וכן קרוב בלשון קדר ואם יש מעט הפרש בין צורת האהל והקבה:


INTO THE CHAMBER. *El ha-kubbah* (into the chamber) means into the tent. The word has a similar meaning in Arabic, although there is a difference in shape between an *ohel* (tent) and a *kubbah* (chamber).¹³ and the Arabic *kubbah*.

footnotes:
¹³ Between what the Hebrews refer to as an ohel


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:2)


**וידקור.** כמו ודקרני בה:


AND THRUST. *Va-yidkor* (and thrust) is similar to *ve-dokreni* (thrust me) in *and thrust me through therewith* (I Sam. 31:4).¹⁴ means and he thrust.

footnotes:
¹⁴ In other words, va-yidkor


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:8:3)


**אל קבתה.** בקובה שהיתה שם עם אחי זמרי. וי"א כי קבתה מגזרת הזרוע והלחיים והקבה וגם יש דרך דרש שנעשו י' נסים לפינחס רק הכתוב לא הזכירם:


THROUGH HER BELLY. *El kovatah* (through her belly) means in the chamber, that is, in the chamber where she was with Zimri’s brothers. Others say that the word that *kovatah* is related to is the word *kevah* (maw) in *the shoulder, and the two cheeks, and the maw* (Deut. 18:3).¹⁵ means her belly. There is also a midrashic statement to the effect that ten miracles were performed for Phinehas.¹⁶ and *Tanchuma* on our Torah portion list 12 miracles. However, Scripture does not mention them.

footnotes:
¹⁵ In other words, kovatah
¹⁶ The Sifre



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:11:1)


**בקנאו.** קל הנו"ן להקל על הלשון והטעם כי הוא קנא כקונו וכתוב על השם כי הוא אל קנא בע"א:


IN THAT HE WAS VERY JEALOUS FOR MY SAKE AMONG THEM. There is no *dagesh* in the *nun* of *be-kano* (in that he was very jealous) in order to simplify enunciation.¹⁷ is in the *pi’el*. The middle root letters in the *pi’el* receive a *dagesh*. Hence I.E.’s comment. It means that Phinehas was jealous like his creator.¹⁸. 38 reads: He acted jealously in My place. Scripture tells us that God is *a jealous God* (Ex. 20:5) when it comes to idolatry.

footnotes:
¹⁷ Kano
¹⁸ Vat. Ebr


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:11:2)


ולולי הוא שקנא הייתי מכלה כל ישראל בדבר **בקנאתי**:


[SO THAT I CONSUMED NOT THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL IN MY JEALOUSY.] If Phinehas had not been jealous for My sake, then I would have destroyed all of Israel in My jealousy.



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:12:1)


**את בריתי שלום.** טעמו את בריתי ברית שלום כמו כסאך אלהים ורבים כן והטעם שלא יגור מאחי זמרי כי הוא נשיא בית אב ושכרו שתהיה לו ולזרעו אחריו ברית כהונת עולם ונצח כי הכהנים הגדולים היו מבני פינחס ויתכן שהיו בנים אחרים לאלעזר:


MY COVENANT OF PEACE. The meaning of *beriti shalom* (My covenant of peace) is:¹⁹ is: My covenant peace. “Of” is not in the Hebrew text. Hence I.E.’s comment. My covenant, a covenant of peace.²⁰ should be read as if written *beriti, berit shalom*. Compare, *Thy throne God*²¹ (Ps. 45:7).²² There are many such cases.²³ Its meaning is that Phinehas should not fear the brothers of Zimri for Zimri²⁴ was a prince of a father’s house (v. 14). Phinehas was rewarded with the covenant of priesthood for himself and his seed forever, for all the high priests were descendants of Phinehas. It is possible that Eleazar²⁵ had other sons.²⁶

footnotes:
¹⁹ The literal meaning of beriti shalom
²⁰ In other words, beriti shalom
²¹ Translated literally.
²² Which should be read as if written, Thy throne, throne of God. See I.E. on Num. 12:6.
²³ Where a word is missing in the Biblical text and has to be supplied by the reader.
²⁴ Who came from a powerful family which might avenge his death.
²⁵ Who served as high priest after the passing of Aaron. See Num. 20:22-29.
²⁶ The high priesthood would in any case have been passed on through Phinehas.



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:13:1)


ומלת **אחריו.** לאות שמת ואינו אליהו כלל וכבר פירשתיו כי נגיד היה עליהם וגם שהיה בימי פילגש בגבעה:


The word *acharav* (after him) (v. 13)²⁷ is proof that Phinehas died and is in no way to be identified with Elijah.²⁸ on the Torah portion, *Phinehas*. I have already explained it.²⁹ Phinehas *was ruler over them* [Israel] (I Chron. 9:20). He was alive during the incident of the concubine from Gibeah.³⁰

footnotes:
²⁷ The phrase “after him” means after his death.
²⁸ Who did not die. Some of the rabbis believe that Phinehas is Elijah. See Yalkut Shimoni
²⁹ See Jud. 20:28.
³⁰ See Judges, chapters 19-21. These citations, which occurred before the story of Elijah, prove that Phinehas could not be Elijah.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:13:2)


**אשר קנא לאלהיו.** מאהבתו את השם גם עשה טובה לישראל ודרך דרש האב כפר ובניו יכפרו:


BECAUSE HE WAS JEALOUS FOR HIS GOD. He acted out of his love for God. He also did good for Israel.³¹. According to the midrashic interpretation, the father³² atoned and the children³³ will atone.³⁴ and *because he…made atonement for the children of Israel*.

footnotes:
³¹ I.E.’s paraphrase of and made atonement for the children of Israel
³² Phinehas.
³³ Phinehas’s descendants.
³⁴ Hence the connection between an everlasting priesthood



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:14:1)


**אשר הכה את המדינית.** עם ורבים כן:


WHO WAS SLAIN WITH THE MIDIANITISH WOMAN. *Et ha-midyanit* is to be rendered, with the Midianitish woman.³⁵ here means with. There are many such instances.³⁶ has the meaning of with. See I.E. on Ex. 1:1 (Vol. 2, p. 3).

footnotes:
³⁵ In other words, et
³⁶ Where et


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:14:2)


**נשיא בית אב.** אחד מחמשת אבות בני שמעון. וראש לבית אבותיו גדול:


A PRINCE OF A FATHER’S HOUSE AMONG THE SIMEONITES. One of the five fathers’ [houses] of the children of Simeon.³⁷ The head of his father’s house was greater than he.³⁸

footnotes:
³⁷ See Num. 26:12,13.
³⁸ The tribe of Simeon was divided into five branches, each of which was called a father’s house. Zimri was a prince of one of the five branches of the tribe of Simeon (Num. 1:4). Midian was similarly divided into five houses.



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:15:1)


**צור.** אחד ממלכי מדין והנה גם מלכי מדין חמשה אבות:


ZUR. Zur was one of the kings of Midian. Note, each of the five kings of Midian ruled over a house.



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:17:1)


**צרור.** שם הפועל כמו זכור את יום השבת:


HARASS. *Tzaror* (harass) is an infinitive.³⁹. An imperative is vocalized with a *sheva*. It is like the word *zakhor*⁴⁰ rather than with a *sheva*. (remember) in *Remember the sabbath day, to keep it holy*.

footnotes:
³⁹ It is vocalized with a kamatz
⁴⁰ Which is also vocalized with a kamatz



### Verse 18

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:18:1)


**כי צוררים הם לכם.** כי הצרו לכם:


FOR THEY HARASS YOU. *Ki tzorerim*⁴¹ is a present form. However, Scripture tells of what the Midianites did to the Israelites. Hence I.E.’s comment. *hem lakhem* (for they harass you) is to be rendered: for they harassed you.

footnotes:
⁴¹ Tzorerim


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/25:18:2)


**בנכליהם.** במחשבתם הרעה מגזרת ויתנכלו אותו:


BY THEIR WILES. *Be-nikhlehem* (by their wiles) means by their evil thoughts. *Be-nikhlehem* is related to the word *va-yitnakkelu* (they conspired) in *they conspired against him* (Gen. 37:18).⁴².

footnotes:
⁴² Both words come from the same root, nun, kaf, lamed


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 25:18:3

**ועל דבר כזבי.** ועוד כי יחשבו לעשות לכם רע בעבור כזבי אחותם:


AND IN THE MATTER OF COZBI. Furthermore, they intend to harm you because of what happened to Cozbi their sister.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:1:1)


**ויהי אחרי המגפה.** אמר השם אל משה ואל אלעזר על יד משה וכן ויצו משה וזקני ישראל אז ישיר משה ובני ישראל:


AND IT CAME TO PASS AFTER THE PLAGUE.¹ God spoke to Moses. He also spoke to Eleazar via Moses.² *And Moses and the elders of Israel commanded the people*³ (Deut. 27:1) and *Then sang Moses and the children of Israel*⁴ (Ex. 15:1) are similar.

footnotes:
¹ This line concludes chapter 25, verse 19, and continues into chapter 26, verse 1.
² I.E.’s interpretation of: That the Lord spoke unto Moses and unto Eleazar the son of Aaron the priest…
³ Its meaning is: Moses commanded the elders, who then commanded Israel.
⁴ Its meaning is: Moses taught the song to the Israelites. See I.E. on Ex. 15:1 (Vol. 2, p. 291).


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:1:2)


וטעם **אחרי המגפה.** בעבור שאמר השם לאלה תחלק הארץ:


Scripture states *after the plague* because God said, *Unto these*⁵ *the land shall be divided* (v. 53).

footnotes:
⁵ Those who survived the plague.



### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:3:1)


**וידבר משה ואלעזר הכהן.** במקום אביו ויתכן שהוא היה פה למשה כמו אהרן:


AND MOSES AND ELEAZAR THE PRIEST SPOKE. In place of his father.⁶ It is possible that Eleazar served as Moses’ spokesman as Aaron did.⁷

footnotes:
⁶ Now that Aaron was dead, Eleazar spoke God’s word to the people.
⁷ He not only served as spokesman in this case but did so in all other cases.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:3:2)


**אתם.** כמו דברו לשלום:


WITH THEM. *Otam* (with them)⁸ means them. Scripture should have read *ittam* (with them). Hence I.E.’s comment. is similar to *dabbero* (speak…unto him) in *speak peaceably unto him* (Gen. 37:4).⁹ is irregular, for it is a compound of *dabber* (speak) and *oto* (him). *Dabbero* literally means speak him. We thus sees that Scripture employs the term “him” with the meaning of “with him.” The same applies to the term *otam* (them). Scripture occasionally employs it with the meaning of “with them.”

footnotes:
⁸ Otam
⁹ Dabbero



### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:4:1)


**מבן עשרים שנה.** והטעם שנשאו את חשבונם כאשר צוה ה' את משה:


FROM TWENTY YEARS OLD AND UPWARD. This means that they took the sum of the people¹⁰ is short for: they took up the sum of the people from twenty years old and up. as God commanded Moses.

footnotes:
¹⁰ In other words, From twenty years old and upward


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:4:2)


וטעם **ובני ישראל היוצאים מארץ מצרים.** כי היו בספורים רבים מיוצאי מצרים והקרוב להיותם חצים והחל מראובן בספורים הראשונים במדבר סיני:


[AND THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL, THAT CAME FORTH OUT OF THE LAND OF EGYPT.] Scripture states the aforementioned because there were many among those who were numbered who came out of Egypt.¹¹ They most likely came to about half the number of the Israelites. Scripture starts with Reuben in imitation of the manner in which the Israelites¹² were first numbered in the wilderness of Sinai.

footnotes:
¹¹ Those who were under the age of twenty when they left Egypt did not die in the wilderness.
¹² See Num. 1:20.

### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:11:1)


**ובני קרח לא מתו.** והעד שמואל ובניו ובני בניו שהם המשוררים והם הנקראים הקרחים לבני קרח מזמור גם בתורה כתיב משפחת הקרחי והזכיר הכתוב ובני קרח עם בני ראובן בעבור כי בני דתן ואבירם גדולים וקטנים מתו והנה רעת דתן ואבירם קשה מרעת קרח:


NOTWITHSTANDING THE SONS OF KORAH DIED NOT. Samuel,¹⁸ his sons, and his grandsons who were singers¹⁹ and were called the Korahites²⁰ is proof of this.²¹ The Book of Psalms states, *For the leader; a Psalm of the sons of Korah* (Ps. 49:1). The Torah also records the family of the Korahites (v. 58). Scripture mentions the sons of Korah with the sons of Reuben because the adult and the minor sons of Dathan and Abiram died. The wickedness of Dathan and Abiram was thus greater than the wickedness of Korah.²²

footnotes:
¹⁸ The prophet Samuel.
¹⁹ In the temple.
²⁰ See I Chron. 6:18-23.
²¹ That Korah’s children did not die.
²² Hence their children died. When Moses asked Dathan and Abiram to come to him, they refused and insulted Moses. We do not find the same with Korah (Num. 16:12-14).



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:12:1)


**הימיני.** אל תבקש דקדוק בשמות וכן מן פוני ומן לשפופם השופמי לימנה משפחת הימנה ואיננו הימיני כמו מן בריעה בריעי וחגי ושוני חסר יו"ד היחס או זה יו"ד היחס ושל השם חסר והוא הנכון ואחר ראובן שמעון וגד החונים עליו רק מנשה קודם אפרים ובמדבר סיני הפוך והנכון שזה בעבור מספרם כי בני אפרים יוסיפו על בני מנשה במדבר סיני שמונת אלפים ושלשת מאות ובערבות מואב יוסיפו בני מנשה על בני אפרים עשרים אלף ומאתים וכן זכר למשפחות חמשה בנים לבנימין והם בכר וגרא ונעמן וראש וארד:


THE JAMINITES. Do not seek grammatical consistency in names. Compare, *puvvah*²³ and *puni* (Punites) (v. 23).²⁴ rather than *puni* to be used for a member of Puvvah’s family. From *shefufam*²⁵ Scripture derives *shufami* (v. 39).²⁶ to be used. Scripture also reads: *of yimnah* (Imnah), *the family of yimnah* (v. 44), not *ha-yemini*²⁷. as it does in the case of *bri’ah*²⁸ and *beri’iy* (Beriites)²⁹ (v. 44); *Chaggi*³⁰ and *shuni* were sons of Gad. Scripture refers to members of their family by the terms *chaggi* and *shuni* rather than *chaggi’iy* or *shuni’iy*. and *shuni* (v. 15) lacking the *yod* gentile.³¹ On the other hand, the *yod* of *chaggi* and *shuni* might be the *yod* gentile and the *yod* of the proper name³² of the names of *chaggi* and *shuni*. is missing. The latter is correct. After Reuben, Scripture lists Simeon and Gad, who pitched next to Reuben.³³ However, Manasseh is mentioned before Ephraim.³⁴ The order is reversed in the wilderness of Sinai.³⁵ It appears that this is due to their number, for the sons of Ephraim were more numerous than the sons of Manasseh in the wilderness of Sinai by eight thousand and three hundred,³⁶., 35). and in the plains of Moab the sons of Manasseh numbered more than the sons of Ephraim by twenty thousand and two hundred.³⁷ No mention is made of the family of the following five sons of Benjamin: Becher, Gera, Naaman, Rosh, and Ard.³⁸

²⁴ We would expect puvvi
²⁵ The son of Benjamin.
²⁶ As the term for a member of his family. However, we would expect the term shefufami
²⁷ Which is the correct grammatical form for a member of the family of yimnah
²⁸ The son of Asher.
²⁹ The family of Beriah.
³⁰ Chaggi
³¹ These terms are thus irregular.
³² The yod
³³ And similarly the other tribes. In other words, Scripture does not list the tribes in the order of their births but by their banners. See Num. 10:11-28.
³⁴ In verses 28-34.
³⁵ In Num. 1:32-35.
³⁶ Hence they were there mentioned first. At the census taken in the wilderness of Sinai, Ephraim numbered 40,500 (Num. 1:33) and Manasseh numbered 32,200 (Ibid
³⁷ Hence they were mentioned first. See verses 34 and 37.
³⁸ See Gen. 46:21.

### Verse 57

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:57:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:57:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:57:1)


**לגרשון.** החל מהבכור והזכיר בנו שהוא לבני ולא הזכיר שמעי ויתכן שלא היו לו בנים או היו לו ומתו בלא בנים. ואחר כן הזכיר החברוני שהוא בן קהת ולא הזכיר עוזיאל ואחר כן בני מררי ושב להזכיר בני יצהר ואחר כן בני עמרם ואיחר אותם בעבור שיבאר מה שאירע לבני בן עמרם:


OF GERSHON. Scripture opens with the first-born.⁵² It mentions Gershon’s son Libni. It does not mention Shimei.⁵³ It is possible that he had no children. On the other hand, Shimei might have had children and they died childless. Scripture then mentions the children of Hebron the son of Kohath.⁵⁴ It does not name Uzziel.⁵⁵ It then mentions the sons of Merari⁵⁶. Mahli and Mushi were sons of Merari. See Ex. 6:19. and goes back and refers to the sons of Itzhar.⁵⁷ Then it mentions the sons of Amram.⁵⁸ Scripture places them last⁵⁹ because it goes on to explain what happened to the grandsons of Amram.⁶⁰

⁵⁸ Amram was Kohath’s first-born. See Ex. 6:18.
⁵⁹ Even though Amram was born before Hebron and Izhar.

### Verse 58

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:58:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:58:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:58:1)


**הקרחי.** הם בני קרח. והזכיר שם יוכבד בעבור כבוד בניה ולא הזכי' שם יולדת' דרך קצר' וכן אשריאל אשר ילדה:


THE KORAHITES. They are the sons of Korah.⁶¹ Scripture mentions Jochebed out of respect to her sons. It does not speak of her mother, because Scripture is being brief.⁶² *Asriel, whom she bore*⁶³ (I Chron. 7:14) is similar.⁶⁴

footnotes:
⁶¹ For Korah was then dead.
⁶² Our verse literally reads: And the name of Amram’s wife was Jochebed, the daughter of Levi, whom she bore. Scripture does not identify the one who did the bearing, i.e., Jochebed’s mother.
⁶³ Translated literally.
⁶⁴ Here too Scripture omits the subject.



### Verse 62

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 26:62:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:62:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/26:62:1)


**ויהיו פקודיהם.** פקודי הלוים במספר הזה נוספו שבע מאות והתימה כי לא נוסף בהם רק שבע מאות והנה ישראל שמתו מי שהיה מבן עשרים שנה ומעלה היה מספרם בפעם האחרונה קרוב מהמספר הראשון כי הלוים לא נכנסו עם פקודי ישראל והעד אלעזר הכהן:


AND THEY THAT WERE NUMBERED. The Levites. The Levites increased by seven hundred in this numbering.⁶⁵ It is amazing that the Levites increased by only seven hundred. Look! The Israelites from among whom all those above the age of twenty died⁶⁶ numbered about the same as they did during their first numbering.⁶⁷ [The small increase in the number of Levites is strange] for the Levites were not included in the numbering of Israel.⁶⁸ Eleazar the Kohen proves the latter.⁶⁹

footnotes:
⁶⁵ The first time the Levites were counted they numbered 22,300. See Num. 3:39 along with I.E.’s comment on it. Their number here is given as 23,000. The Levites thus increased by 700 in close to 40 years.
⁶⁶ By the time the census recorded in our chapter was taken. See verse 64.
⁶⁷ The number of Israelites at the first census came to 603,550 (Num. 1:46). They now numbered 601,730.
⁶⁸ By God’s decree only those above the age of 20 who were numbered in the wilderness of Sinai had died by the time of the second census. See verse 64 and Num. 14:29. This decree did not fall upon the Levites.
⁶⁹ Had the Levites been included in the numbering, Eleazar too would be dead, for he was most likely above the age of twenty when the first census was taken.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:3:1

AND HE WAS NOT AMONG THE COMPANY. The daughters¹ thought that the children of those who gathered themselves together against the Lord² would not inherit.³

¹ Of Zelophehad.
² In the company of Korah (Num. 16:1-19).
³ Hence they said that their father was not in Korah’s company.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:3:2)


**כי בחטאו מת.** אמר ר' יהודה הלוי הספרדי כי פי' כי בחטאו מת דבק עם ובנים לא היו לו כאשר יאמר היום בעונותיו אירע לפלוני כך וכך ואיננו רחוק:


BUT HE DIED IN HIS OWN SIN. Rabbi Judah Ha-Levi the Spaniard says *but he died in his own sin* is to be understood as connected to *and he had no sons*.⁴ It is as we say today, such and such occurred to so and so because of his sins. This interpretation is not far off.

footnotes:
⁴ In other words, the meaning here is: he died without sons because of his sins.

BECAUSE HE HAD NO SON. *Ki en lo ben* is to be rendered, because he had no son.⁵ in our verse is because. *Ki* has a number of meanings. Hence I.E.’s comments.

⁵ In other words, the meaning of ki

RIGHT. *Ken* means true.⁶). Or, the thing is so.⁷) as the daughters of Zelophehad speak.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:8:1

UNTO HIS BRETHREN. From his father or mother.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:11:1)


**לשארו הקרוב אליו.** מכל המשפחה:


UNTO HIS KINSMAN THAT IS NEXT TO HIM. From all of his family.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ That is closest to the deceased.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:11:2)


**וירש אותה.** הנחלה וכבר פירשתי דרך חז"ל בפסוק לעם נכרי. ולדעת הנחלו' אם יירש האב או האם את הבן ומה שתירש אשתו צריכים אנחנו לקבלה:


AND HE SHALL POSSESS IT. The inheritance. I have already explained the approach of the rabbis in my comments on *unto a foreign people* (Ex. 21:8).⁹. The rabbis render this, and he (the husband) shall inherit her (*otah*). According to the rabbis our verse teaches that a man inherits his wife’s property but a woman does not inherit her husband’s property. See *Baba Batra* 111b and I.E.’s short commentary on Ex. 21:8. We need rabbinic tradition to know the laws of inheritance with regard to whether a father or a mother inherits a son’s property or what a woman inherits.

footnotes:
⁹ Our verse reads, ve-yarash otah



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:12:1)


אמרו חז"ל כי נסמכה פרשת עלה אל הר העברים לדעת הנחלות כי בקש משה לדעת מי ינחל מעלתו:


The rabbis of blessed memory said that the chapter that opens with the words, *Get thee up into this mountain* (v. 12), follows the laws of inheritance because Moses wanted to know who would inherit his position.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/27:14:1)


**להקדישני.** דבק עם מריתם והטעם כי מריתם להקדישני וזה הוא על אשר לא קדשתם כאשר פירש:


TO SANCTIFY ME. The word *le-hakdesheni* (to sanctify me) is connected to *meritem* (ye rebelled).¹⁰ Its meaning is, ye rebelled with regard to sanctifying me.¹¹ This, I have explained,¹² is the meaning of *because ye sanctified Me not in the midst of the children of Israel* (Deut. 32:51).

¹⁰ Even though these words do not follow each other.
¹¹ Its meaning is, you rebelled against me by not sanctifying me.

ה' **אלהי הרוחות.** שהוא יודע הרוחות איזה רוח ראוי:


THE GOD OF THE SPIRITS. He knows the spirits. He knows which spirit is fit.¹³

¹³ To lead the congregation.

[SET.] The word *yifkod* (set) is related to the word *pakid* (official).¹⁴ means let Him appoint a leader.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:17:1

WHO MAY GO OUT BEFORE THEM. In war.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 27:18:1

IN WHOM IS SPIRIT. Now all living persons have spirit in them.¹⁶? Its meaning can only be similar to that of *be thou strong therefore, and show thyself a man* (I Kings 2:2).¹⁷

## Chapter 28


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:1:1)


וטעם הדבק פרשת הקרבנות שהשם צוה אז למשה שיצוה את בני ישראל הקרבנות שהם חייבים לעשות כי הוא לא יכנס לארץ עמהם והחל להזכיר עולת תמיד ואם הזכירה בתחלה יתכן שהנזכרת היא על הר סיני או שתהיה מערכת הקרבנות סדורה:


The reason the chapter dealing with the sacrifices follows²¹ is that God then²² ordered Moses to command the children of Israel regarding the sacrifices they are obligated to offer,²³ for Moses was not to enter the land with them. Scripture starts with the daily burnt offering even though it was mentioned earlier.²⁴ It is possible that the earlier burnt offering was the one offered at Mount Sinai.²⁵ On the other hand, it is possible that Scripture wants to insure that the arrangement of the sacrifices is in order.²⁶

footnotes:
²¹ The chapter dealing with the appointment of Joshua to succeed Moses.
²² Following the appointment of Joshua.
²³ In the Land of Israel.
²⁴ In Ex. 29:42. The point is, why repeat the law of the burnt offering.
²⁵ When Israel camped around Mount Sinai. In other words, it does not pertain to the daily burnt offering to be made when they entered the Land of Israel.
²⁶ Scripture repeats the law of the burnt offering here because it wants to list in order all of the burnt offerings which the congregation is obligated to bring, i.e., the daily burnt offering, the new moon burnt offering, the Passover offering, etc.



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:1)


**לחמי.** פירשתיו:


MY FOOD. I have previously explained the meaning of the term *lechem* (food).¹ can refer to bread, meat, or fruit. See I.E. on Ex. 16:4 (Vol. 2, p. 316).

footnotes:
¹ In his comments on Ex. 16:4, I.E. explains that the term lechem


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:2)


**לאשי.** פירשתיו. והטעם לחם לאשי לריח ניחוחי:


MADE BY FIRE. I have previously explained the meaning of *le-ishai* (made by fire).² means a burnt offering. Thus *le-ishai* means for my burnt offerings. The meaning of *lachmi le-ishai re’ach nicho’ach* (My food…made by fire, of a sweet savour unto Me) is, food for my fire, for a sweet savour unto Me.

footnotes:
² According to I.E. the word isheh


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:2:3)


**במועדו.** שלא יקדמו קודם הבקר ושלא יאחר אחר הערב:


IN ITS DUE SEASON. It shall not be bought before the morning, nor delayed after evening.



### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:4:1)


**את הכבש אחד.** אמר רבי משה הכהן הספרדי כי הוא דרך קצרה והטעם את הכבש כבש א' ועל דעתי שאות ה"א מושך עצמו ואחר עמו וכבש הראיתיך רבים כן:


THE ONE LAMB. Rabbi Moses Ha-Kohen the Spaniard says that our verse is abridged. It should be read as if written *ha-keves, keves echad* (the lamb, one lamb).³ (the one lamb). A word with the direct object prefixed to it cannot be in the construct. Our verse reads, the lamb one in the morning. This is incorrect syntax. Hence Rabbi Moses’ interpretation. However, I believe that the *heh* of *keves* is to be read as if written twice.⁴. I have already shown you many such cases.⁵

footnotes:
³ Our verse reads, et ha-keves echad
⁴ Our verse is to be read as if written, et ha-keves, keves echad ba-boker
⁵ Where a letter is to be read as if written twice.



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:6:1)


**העשויה בהר סיני.** הטעם כאשר עשיתם בהר סיני גם זה אות שלא העלו עולות במדבר אחר נסעם מסיני כאשר פירשתי בראיות גמורות:


WHICH WAS OFFERED IN MOUNT SINAI. Its meaning is, as you offered on Mount Sinai.⁶ This verse⁷ is an additional proof that Israel did not offer burnt offerings in the wilderness after they journeyed from Sinai,⁸ as I previously explained with perfect proof.⁹

footnotes:
⁶ In other words, they were not to bring the very same offering which they had brought at Sinai, for that offering no longer existed. They were to bring a similar offering.
⁷ In addition to Ex. 29:42. See I.E. on Ex. 29:42 (Vol. 2, p. 620).
⁸ Our verse speaks of the burnt offering at Sinai. It does not speak of the one offered in the wilderness. This implies that no burnt offering was offered in the wilderness.
⁹ See I.E. on Ex. 29:42.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:7:1)


**הסך נסך שכר.** נו"ן השורש מובלע בסמ"ך ומן הפירוש נסמוך על דברי הקדמונים ז"ל:


SHALT THOU POUR OUT A DRINK-OFFERING OF STRONG DRINK. The stem *nun*¹⁰, the root of *hassekh* (shalt thou pour out). is swallowed by the *samekh*.¹¹ (shalt thou pour out). Hence the *dagesh* in the *samekh*. We will rely on the words of our sages of blessed memory as to its meaning.¹² (strong drink). According to the sages it refers to undiluted wine. In ancient times wine for drinking was mixed with water.

footnotes:
¹⁰ Of the root nun, samekh, caf
¹¹ In the word hassekh
¹² We will rely on the sages concerning what Scripture means by the term nesekh



### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:10:1)


**בשבתו.** שב אל יום השבת אע"פ שנמצא כל שומר שבת מחללו והטעם כל שבת ושבת כמו יום ביומו:


OF EVERY SABBATH. The *vav* suffixed to the word *shabbat*¹³ in the word *shabbato* (every Sabbath). refers to the day of Sabbath.¹⁴ is feminine. Thus Scripture should have used the term *shabbatah* if the reference was to every Sabbath. Hence I.E.’s comment that the *vav* refers to the word *yom* in verse 9, which is masculine. Thus according to I.E. we should render *shabbato* (every Sabbath) as every Sabbath day. This is so even though we find the word *me-challelo*¹⁵. (from profaning it¹⁶ as masculine.) in *Every one that keepeth the sabbath from profaning it* (Is. 56:6). The meaning of *be-shabbato* (of every Sabbath) is, *every sabbath and sabbath*. Compare, *yom be-yomo* (daily) (Ex. 5:13).

footnotes:
¹³ The reference is to the vav
¹⁴ Shabbat
¹⁵ Rather than mechallelah
¹⁶ The reference is to the Sabbath. We thus see that Scripture treats shabbat

**על עולת התמיד.** הטעם אחרי התמיד שישים עליה עולת שבת:


BESIDE THE CONTINUAL BURNT-OFFERING. This means after the continual burnt offering. The Sabbath burnt offering shall follow it.¹⁷

### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:11:1)


**ובראשי חדשיכם.** אמר רבי משה הכהן הספרדי נ"ע שפירושו חדש ניסן כי כן כתוב ראשון הוא לכם ואחר כן אמר זאת עולת חדש בחדשו שיעשו כן בכל חדש על כן הוסיף לחדשי השנה ופירושו נכון הוא ואע"פ שיש לטעון על יו"ד ובראשי חדשיכם רק בעבור שמצאנו הנה חדש מחר ישר דבורו:


AND IN YOUR NEW MOONS. Rabbi Moses Ha-Kohen the Spaniard, who rests in paradise, says that *u-ve-roshe chodshekhem*¹⁸ (and in your new moons) refers to the new moon of Nisan, for Scripture clearly states, *it shall be the first month of the year to you* (Ex. 12:2).¹⁹ Scripture then goes on to say, *This is the burnt-offering of every new moon* (v. 14), that is, they shall offer this burnt offering²⁰ on every new moon. The Torah therefore adds, *throughout the months of the year* (*Ibid.*).²¹ Rabbi Moses’ interpretation is correct. He is right even though the *yod* in *u-ve-roshe chodshekhem* (and in your new moons) argues against his interpretation.²² is a plural. Hence *roshe chodshekhem* seems to mean the many first days of the month. If the reference was to the new moon of Nisan, then Scripture should have read *u-ve-rosh chodshekem* (and on the first day of your months). However, the fact that we find *hinneh chodesh machar*²³. (Behold, tomorrow is the new moon) (I Sam. 20:5) shows that what he says is correct.²⁴. This implies that an ordinary new moon is called *chodesh*, and the new moon of Nisan *rosh chodesh*. Otherwise Samuel would have referred to the new moon by the phrase *rosh chodesh*.

footnotes:
¹⁸ Literally, and in the first of your months. Hence Rabbi Moses’ interpretation.
¹⁹ Hence the first of your months (see previous note) refers to the first month of the year, i.e., the new moon of Nisan.
²⁰ The same burnt offering that was offered on the new moon of Nisan.
²¹ A similar burnt offering shall be offered on the new moons throughout the year.
²² Roshe
²³ Not hinneh rosh chodesh machar
²⁴ The Book of Samuel refers to the new moon as chodesh



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:14:1)


**בחדשו.** כמו שבת בשבתו ולא יתכן בו על דרך הפשט פי' אחר כי אין בפסוק זכר לירח ועוד כי לא מצאנו זאת המלה כי אם מהבנין הכבד הדגוש והנה הדל"ת רפה:


OF EVERY NEW MOON. *Chodesh be-chodsho* (of every new moon) is similar²⁵ to *shabbat be-shabbato* (of every sabbath) (Num. 28 :10).²⁶ is every new moon. According to the plain meaning of the text there is no possibility of interpreting it any other way,²⁷ to mean when it (the moon) renews itself. for there is no mention of the moon in our verse.²⁸ to mean new and *chodesh be-chodsho* to mean as when it renews itself, then the subject (the word moon) is missing from the text. Furthermore, we only find the Hebrew form for the verb new in the *pi’el*, which has a *dagesh* placed in it.²⁹, not *chodesh be-chodsho*. However, here³⁰. the *dalet* has no *dagesh* in it.

### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:15:1)


**ושעיר עזים אחד לחטאת לה'.** כמו אשר עלה עליו הגורל לה' ועשהו חטאת. ודע כי חטאת עם למ"ד בלשון הקדש אין החטאת דבק כי אם בחוטא הלא תראה וחטאתי לאבי והנה זה הפך הדרש רק יש לו סוד:


AND ONE HE-GOAT FOR A SIN-OFFERING UNTO THE LORD. Compare, *upon which the lot fell for the Lord, and offer him for a sin-offering* (Lev. 16:9).³¹ Note, in Hebrew the sin is connected to the sinner whenever the word *chattat* is followed by a *lamed*.³² prefaced to a word sometimes means of. Compare, *mizmor le-david* (a psalm of David). Thus the phrase *chattat la-Adonai* might be taken to mean a sin of God. Therefore I.E. points out that its meaning is, an offering to God to atone for a sin. Can’t you see that Scripture reads, *ve-chatati le-avi* ³³ literally means I will sin to my father. (I shall bear the blame to my father) (Gen. 44:32). Look, this is contrary to the midrashic interpretation.³⁴ (for a sin-offering unto the Lord), which literally states, for a sin offering for the Lord, means that the sin offering is offered on behalf of God for diminishing the size of the moon. See *Shavu’ot* 9a. However, the midrashic interpretation has a secret meaning.³⁵ he indicates that the sin offering is to avert the unfavorable consequences which come about when the moon is full. See Meijler on this verse.

³² The lamed
³³ Ve-chatati le-avi
³⁴ According to the midrash, le-chattat la-Adonai
³⁵ I.E. does not give us its secret meaning. However, in his Yesod Ha-Ibbur

### Verse 22

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 28:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/28:22:1)


**ושעיר חטאת.** כמו שעיר עזים והוא דרך קצרה והנה לא הזכיר עולות בשבעת ימי הפסח כאשר לא הזכיר עולת התנופה ואנו צריכין לקבלה כי פסוק שבת בשבתו אמר על עולת התמיד:


AND ONE HE-GOAT FOR A SIN-OFFERING. *U-se’ir chattat echad* (and he-goat for a sin-offering) is the same as *u-se’ir izzim echad le-chattat*³⁸ (and one he-goat for a sin-offering). Our verse is abridged.³⁹ is short for *se’ir izzim*. Observe, Scripture does not mention the Sabbath burnt offering⁴⁰. 38. Also see Krinsky and Meijler. The printed texts omit the word Sabbath. which is to be brought during the seven days of Passover. It similarly omits⁴¹ the burnt offering which accompanied the waved sheaf.⁴² We need tradition.⁴³ For the verse (v. 10) tells us that [the burnt offering] *of every sabbath* shall follow the daily burnt offering.⁴⁴

**בהקריבכם מנחה חדשה.** לחם התנופה הוא העיקר ועמו שבעה כבשים ופר בן בקר ואילים שני' ושני כבשי שלמים והעולה הנזכרת בפרשה הזאת היא חובת היום והעד על דבריהם כי לא באר בשבת ובמועד דבר כלל רק הזכיר בחג המצות וסכות ויום העשור והקרבתם אשה לה' ולא אמר כמה המספר וכן ביום תרועה ולא הזכיר עולת חדש ועולת התמיד ומנחתה היא העולה הראשונה:


WHEN YE BRING A NEW MEAL-OFFERING UNTO THE LORD. The two waveloaves⁴⁵. See Lev. 23:17-21. were the main offerings.⁴⁶ The seven lambs, one young bullock, two rams and two lambs of peace offering⁴⁷ were brought along with it. The burnt offering mentioned in our chapter⁴⁸ refers to the obligatory sacrifice of the festival day.⁴⁹ The fact that Scripture does not state⁵⁰ anything at all⁵¹ about the Sabbath and festivals, but only mentions *And ye shall bring an offering made by fire* without telling us the number of sacrificial animals for the festival of unleavened bread (Lev. 23:8), and Sukkot (Lev. 23:36), and the holiday celebrated on the tenth day of Tishri (Lev. 23:27) is proof of the words of our sages.⁵² The same is true for the day of the shofar sounding.⁵³ Scripture does not mention the new moon burnt offering.⁵⁴ *And the continual burnt-offering and the meal-offering thereof* (Num. 29:6) refers to the first burnt offering.⁵⁵

## Chapter 29


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 29:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/29:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/29:1:1)


**יום תרועה.** מצוה להריע כי אילו היה זה הדיבור על עולת היום למה לא הזכיר בשאר המועדים ועוד כי כל העולות צוה לתקוע ולא להריע:


IT IS A DAY OF BLOWING THE HORN.¹. Literally, a day of *tru’ah*. I.E.’s interpretation of *yom tru’ah* is, a day of sounding a *tru’ah*. It is a commandment to sound a *tru’ah*.² is a wavering blast. If this statement referred to the burnt offering offered on this day,³ then why is it not mentioned with regard to the other festivals?⁴ was to the sound to be played over the burnt offerings on the festivals cited in Num. 10:10, then the Torah should have mentioned the sounding of the *tru’ah* with regard to the other festivals. Furthermore, the Torah tells us to sound a *teki’ah*⁵ over the burnt offerings, not a *tru’ah*.⁶. If the reference was to the sound played over the burnt offerings, the Torah would have spoken of a *teki’ah*.

⁶ Our verse speaks of a tru’ah



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 29:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/29:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/29:8:1)


**פר בן בקר אחד.** של צבור וכן איל אחד ואינם פר כהן גדול והאיל שלו ולפי דעתי כי האיל הנזכר בפרשת אחרי מות שלקח מן העם איננו זה כי על אילו ואיל העם נאמר ועשה את עולתו ואת עולת העם והעד שיש תוספת שבעת כבשים גם זה שעיר עזים אחד לחטאת איננו משעיר הגורלות על כן כתוב מלבד חטאת הכפורים הנכתבת בפרשת אחרי מות:


ONE YOUNG BULLOCK. Brought by the congregation. The same applies to one ram. The reference is not to the bullock and ram offered by the *kohen gadol* (high priest).⁷I believe that the ram mentioned in the Torah portion *Achare mot,*⁸ which the *kohen* took from the people,⁹ is not the ram mentioned in our verse, for Scripture says, with regard to the high priest’s ram and the ram of the people, *and offer his burnt-offering and the burnt-offering of the people* (Lev. 16:24).¹⁰ (the burnt offering of the people). The fact that there are seven additional lambs¹¹ is proof of this.¹² in our chapter was accompanied by seven lambs, while the ram spoken of in Leviticus was not so accompanied. The one he-goat for a sin offering¹³ is not to be identified with one of the he-goats chosen by lot.¹⁴ Scripture therefore states,¹⁵ *beside the sin-offering of atonement* (v. 11) mentioned in the Torah portion *Achare mot*.¹⁶ (v. 11) is, beside the sin offering (the he-goat) mentioned in the Torah portion *Achare mot*.

## Chapter 30


### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:2:1)


**ראשי המטות.** לפי דעתי שזאת הפרשה היתה אחר מלחמת מדין ע"כ היא אחריה כמו ותדבר מרים ואהרן במשה אחר ויהיו בחצרות כאשר פירשתי והכ' אמר שבאו בני גד אל משה ואל אלעזר ואל נשיאי העדה ודכרו דבריהם ואחרי כן כתוב ויצו להם משה את אלעזר הכהן ואת יהושע בן נון ואת ראשי אבות המטות הם נשיאי העדה הנזכרים ובעבור שאמר משה לבני גד ולבני ראובן והיוצא מפיכם תעשו ע"כ כתוב וידבר משה אל ראשי המטות ושם כתוב ככל היוצא מפיו יעשה:


UNTO THE HEADS OF THE TRIBES. I believe that this section¹ followed the war with Midian.² The Midianite war³ therefore comes after it.⁴ Compare, *And Miriam and Aaron spoke against Moses* (Num. 12:1), which comes after *and they abode in Hazeroth* (Num. 11:35)⁵ as I explained. Scripture tells us that the children of Gad came to Moses and Eleazar and the princes of the congregation, and spoke their piece (Num. 32:2-6). Scripture then writes, *So Moses gave charge concerning them to Eleazar the priest, and to Joshua the son of Nun, and to the heads of the fathers’ houses* (Num. 32:28). The *heads of the fathers’* houses refers to the already-mentioned princes (Num. 32:2).⁶ Moses told the children of Gad and the children of Reuben, *and do that which hath proceeded out of your mouth* (Num. 32:24). Scripture therefore reads,⁷ *And Moses spoke unto the heads of the tribes*.⁸ *saying…*(v. 2). *When a man voweth a vow…he shall not break his word*. Scripture there⁹ writes, *he shall do according to all that proceedeth out of his mouth* (v. 3).

### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:4:1)


**ואסרה אסר.** או אסרה איסר. ואסרה ממשקל אחר ואיננו כמו אסר:


AND BINDETH HERSELF BY A BOND. Or bindeth herself by a bond.¹⁴ of *ve-aserah* means or, not and.[5.] HER BOND. *Essarah* (her bond) belongs to a different form.¹⁵. It is not the same as *issar* (bond).¹⁶ has a *dagesh* in the *samekh*. In other words, *esar* and *issar* come from the same root but belong to different forms.

footnotes:
¹⁴ The vav
¹⁵ From issar
¹⁶ For issar



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6:1)


**הניא.** כמו שבר והפר כמו את תנואתי:


DISALLOW. The word *heni* (disallow) means broke or nullified. Compare, *tenu’ati* (My displeasure) (Num. 14:34).


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6:2)


**ביום שמעו.** לא ביום אחר:


IN THE DAY THAT HE HEARETH. And not on any other day.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:7:1)


**ונדריה עליה.** בבית אביה ולא הניא אותה אביה בעלה יניא אותה:


WHILE HER VOWS ARE UPON HER. In her father’s house. If her father does not nullify her vows, her husband may disallow her vows.



### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9:1)


**ביום שמוע.** הדבר מפיה:


HE HEARETH IT. The word from her mouth.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9:2)


**מבטא שפתיה.** השבועה:


AND THE CLEAR UTTERANCE OF HER LIPS. The oath.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:11:1)


**ואם בית אישה נדרה.** האלמנה בימים הקדמונים והניא אותה לא תקיים נדרה בימי אלמנותה וזה על דבר העתיד שנדרה ומת הבעל קודם הגעת הזמן. מלת סליחה מפורשת:


AND IF A WOMAN VOWED IN HER HUSBAND’S HOUSE. If the widow vowed in an earlier time and her husband disallowed it, then she does not have to keep the vow in her widowhood. This speaks about a vow which she vowed regarding the future, and the husband died before the vow was to take effect.¹⁷ The meaning of *selichah* (forgiveness) has been previously explained.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁷ Otherwise our verse would be repeating verse 9. For if a husband can annul his wife’s vow which was made when she was under her father’s charge, he can certainly annul avow made under his charge. Hence I.E. explains that our verse deals with a divorcee or widow who uttered a vow while married and had the vow nullified by her husband, who died before the vow became due. Our verse teaches that in such a case the nullification of the vow retains its efficacy.
¹⁸ See I.E. on Num. 14:19.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:14:1)


**לענות נפש.** אפי' לענות נפש שהוא הצום כאשר פירשתי:


TO AFFLICT THE SOUL. Even to afflict the soul. I have already explained that “affliction of the soul” refers to fasting.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ See I.E. on Lev. 16:29.



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:15:1)


**והקים.** כבר הקים ואחר כן פירש איך הקים אותם בעבור שהחריש ביום שמעו:


THEN HE CAUSETH ALL HER VOWS TO STAND. He has already caused all of her vows to stand.²⁰ means: has already caused, rather than: and he will cause. Scripture then explains how he caused her vows to stand, namely, because he held his peace in the day that he heard them.

footnotes:
²⁰ In other words, ve-hekim



### Verse 16

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:16:1)


**ונשא את עונה.** כי היא ברשותו:


THEN HE SHALL BEAR HER INIQUITY. For she is under his care.



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17:1)


**בין איש לאשתו.** והיא בוגרת:


BETWEEN A MAN AND HIS WIFE. His wife being a woman who is an adult.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 30:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17:2)


**בין אב לבתו.** אם איננה בוגרת על כן נאמר בנעוריה:


BETWEEN A FATHER AND HIS DAUGHTER. If she has not reached adulthood. Hence Scripture reads, *being in her youth*.



## Chapter 31


### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3:1)


**החלצו.** חגרו חלוציכם:


ARM YE MEN. The meaning of *hechaletzu* (arm ye men) is, gird your men of war.¹. 38 reads gird your loins. See Job 38:3 and Is. 5:27. The word *hechaletzu* can also be rendered as strip. Hence I.E.’s comment (Meijler).

footnotes:
¹ Gird a sword upon your men of war (Krinsky). Or, gird the loins of your men of war. Vat. Ebr


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3:2)


**נקמת ה'.** היא נקמת בני ישראל בעבור עבודת כוכבים:


TO EXECUTE THE LORD’S VENGEANCE. The reference is to Israel’s vengeance². However, verse 3 states, *to execute the Lord’s vengeance on Midian*. Hence I.E.’s comment. because they were enticed³ into worshipping idols.

footnotes:
² Verse 2 says, Avenge the children of Israel of the Midianites
³ By the Midianites. See Num. 25:6 and Num. 31:15,16.



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:5:1)


**וימסרו.** כמו וינתנו וקרוב ממנו למסור מעל בה':


SO WERE DELIVERED. *Va-yimasseru* means and they were delivered. The word *li-mesor*⁴ (to revolt so as to break faith). (to deliver) (v. 16) is close in meaning to it.

footnotes:
⁴ Translated literally. Verse 16 reads, li-mesor ma’al



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:6:1

**וכלי הקדש.** הארון:


WITH THE HOLY VESSELS. The ark.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:8:1

AND ZUR. The father of Cozbi.⁶

**בלעם בן בעור.** י"א אחר ששב אל מקומו בא אל מדין כאשר שמע המגפה שהיתה בישראל בעצתו שב לקחת ממון מזקני מדין:


BALAAM ALSO THE SON OF BEOR. Some say that after Balaam returned to his place,⁷ he went back to Midian as soon as he learned of the plague that struck Israel because of his advice. He came back to receive money from the elders of Midian.⁸. 106a.

### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:9:1)


**חילם.** שם כלל והוא זהב וכסף ונחשת וברזל ובגדים:


AND ALL THEIR GOODS. The word *chelam* (their goods) is a general term. It refers to gold, silver, copper, iron, and garments.



### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:10:1)

AND ALL THEIR CITIES IN THE PLACES WHEREIN THEY DWELT. An all-inclusive term⁹ followed by a limiting term.¹⁰ (the places wherein they dwelt).

AND ALL THEIR ENCAMPMENTS. *Tirotam* (their encampments) means the palaces of the kings.¹¹ means their palaces.

### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:11:1)


**ויקחו את כל השלל ואת כל המלקוח.** שם כלל לאדם ולבהמה:


AND THEY TOOK ALL THE SPOIL, AND ALL THE PREY. *Malko’ach* (prey) is a general term for men and cattle.



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:12:1)


**השבי.** פרט לאדם:


THE CAPTIVES. *Shevi* (captive) is a specific term. It refers to humans.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:12:2)


**השלל.** הם הבגדים:


THE SPOIL. *Shalal* (spoil) refers to garments.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:14:1)


**על פקודי החיל.** מגזרת יפקוד ה' כמו פקידי החיל וכן מלת קרואי כמו קריאי:


WITH THE OFFICERS OF THE HOST. The word *pekudai* (officers of) is related to the word *yifkod* (set) in *Let the Lord…set* (Num. 27 :16).¹² (the officers of the host) are those who are set over the host. It¹³. is similar to *pekide he-chayil*.¹⁴ Similarly, the word *keru’e* (the elect of) (Num. 1:16) means the same as *keri’e* (the elect of) (Num. 16:2).

footnotes:
¹² The pekudai he-chayil
¹³ Pekudai he-chayil
¹⁴ So Vat. Ebr. 38.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:14:2)


**שרי האלפים ושרי המאות.** הם מאה ושלשים ושנים:


THE CAPTAINS OF THOUSANDS AND THE CAPTAINS OF HUNDREDS. They totaled one hundred and thirty-two.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ There were 12 captains of thousands and 120 captains of hundreds over the 12,000 Israelites who went to war.



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:15:1)


**החייתם כל נקבה.** הטעם אפי' אחת וכן אין כל ויתכן להיות כמשמעו כי כל נקבה החיו ורבים כן:


HAVE YE SAVED ALL THE WOMEN ALIVE. The meaning of *kol nekevah* (all the women) is, even one woman.¹⁶ (all) sometimes means a part of. *En kol* (had nothing)¹⁷ does not always mean all things. (II Sam. 12:3)¹⁸ is similar. It is also possible that *have ye saved all the women alive* is to be taken literally, for they kept all the females alive. There are many such instances.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁶ In other words, the word kol
¹⁷ It does not mean did not have anything, for he had a lamb. We thus see that the word kol
¹⁸ The reference is to a poor person who had only a lamb.
¹⁹ Where a rhetorical question is raised.



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:17:1)


**טף.** שם כלל לקטן זכר או נקבה:


THE LITTLE ONES. *Taf* (little ones) is a general term for male and female minors.



### Verse 19

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:19:1)


**אתם ושביכם.** בעבור הכבוד השוכן בתוכם:


YE AND YOUR CAPTIVES.²⁰ Because of the glory which dwells among you.

footnotes:
²⁰ Shall encamp outside of the camp until you are purified.



### Verse 20

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:20:1)


**וכל מעשה עזים.** כאשר פירשתי. והנה הבגד בעבור שנגע בחלל הוא טמא הטעם ועמכם כל בגד עד שתתחטאו או תהיה מלת תתחטאו יוצאה וכן והתאויתם לכם כי הגבול הוא הפעול:


AND ALL WORK OF GOATS’ HAIR.²¹. Literally, goats’ work. Hence I.E.’s comments. This is to be interpreted in accordance with my earlier explanation.²² (goats) is to be rendered as goats’ hair.” In other words, “goats’ work” means a garment made out of goats’ hair. Observe, the garment²³ is unclean because it touched a corpse. The meaning of our verse is, the garments shall be with you²⁴ (and as to every garment) is, and with you all garments. I.E. reads our verse as follows: And all garments taken in spoil…[shall be with you outside of the camp] until you are purified. I.E. interprets thus because *titchate’u* is in the *hitpa’el*. Words in the *hitpa’el* are reflexive. until you are purified.²⁵ (ye shall purify) as if written *ad she-titchatu’e* (until you are purified). On the other hand, *titchate’u* (ye shall purify) might be transitive.²⁶ means ye shall purify, i.e., the garments. *Ve-hitavvitem*²⁷. *lakhem* (and ye shall mark out) (Num. 34:10) is similar,²⁸. for *gevul* (line)²⁹ is the object.³⁰ (and ye shall mark out) is transitive.

footnotes:
²¹ Hebrew, ma’aseh izzim
²² See I.E. on Ex. 25:4 (Vol. 2, p. 534) “Izzim
²³ Mentioned in our verse.
²⁴ In other words, the meaning of ve-khol beged
²⁵ I.E. interprets titchate’u
²⁶ In other words, titchate’u
²⁷ It too is in the hitpa’el
²⁸ For it too is transitive even though it is in the hitpa’el
²⁹ Which follows “and ye shall mark out.”
³⁰ Hence ve-hitavvitem



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:21:1)


**ויאמר אלעזר הכהן.** כי חקת תורת הפרה לאלעזר נתנה ומשה אמר להם תתחטאו דרך כלל ואלעזר פירש להם:


AND ELEAZAR THE PRIEST SAID. For the law regarding the cow³¹ was given to Eleazar.³² Moses told the people in a general manner, *ye shall purify* (v. 20). However, Eleazar explained it to them.³³

³¹ The red heifer.
³² See Num. 19:3.
³³ In detail.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:22:1)

### Verse 23

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:23:1)


**אך במי נדה יתחטא.** היה נראה לנו כי הוא מי אפר הפרה כמו מי נדה לא זורק עליו וחז"ל אמרו כי טעמו בשיעור המים שתרחץ בהם האשה הנדה ודעתם רחבה מדעתינו:


NEVERTHELESS IT SHALL BE PURIFIED WITH THE WATER OF SPRINKLING.³⁵. It would have appeared to us that the reference is to the mixture of the water and tahe ashes of the cow,³⁶ as in *because the water of sprinkling*³⁷. *was not dashed against him* (Num. 19:13). However, our wise men of blessed memory say that its meaning is, in the amount of water in which a menstruant bathes.³⁸ 75b. Their mind is much greater than our minds.

footnotes:
³⁵ Hebrew, me niddah
³⁶ The red heifer.
³⁷ Hebrew, me-niddah
³⁸ The utensils are to be immersed in a pool of water which is fit for a menstruant to purify herself. See Avodah Zara

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:26:4)


**וראשי אבות העדה.** הם הנשיאים:


AND THE HEADS OF THE FATHERS’ HOUSES OF THE CONGREGATION. The reference is to the princes.⁴²

footnotes:
⁴² Of the tribes.



### Verse 27

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:27:1)

AND DIVIDE THE PREY. *Malko’ach* (prey) is a general term.⁴³

footnotes:
⁴³ For humans and cattle. See I.E.’s comment on verse 11.



### Verse 28

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:28:1)


**אחד נפש.** כמו נפש אחד:


ONE SOUL. *Echad nefesh* means the same as *nefesh echad* (one soul).⁴⁴ is the usual form. Hence I.E.’s comment.

footnotes:
⁴⁴ Nefesh echad



### Verse 30

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:30:1)


**אחד אחוז.** כמו והאחוז אחוז ושניהם דבקים כאשר פירשתי:


ONE DRAWN. *Achuz* (drawn) is similar to *ve-ha’achuz achuz* (and proportionally) (I Chron. 24:6).⁴⁵, slightly different from what I.E. quotes. As I have explained,⁴⁶ they are connected.⁴⁷ is to be interpreted as one word (Filwarg). Some interpret: verse 28 and 30 are connected to each other. See Weiser.

footnotes:
⁴⁵ The reading in I Chron. 24:6 is ve-achuz achuz
⁴⁶ It isn’t clear where.
⁴⁷ Ve-achuz achuz


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:30:2)


**מכל הבהמה.** כמו גמלים והנה לא לקח אלעזר מהם חלק אולי היו מעטים ועל כן לא נזכר המספר שהביאו ולא המספר שלקחו הלוים:


EVEN OF ALL THE CATTLE. Camels.⁴⁸ seems to add something new. Since cattle, flock, asses are clearly mentioned in our verse, I.E. reasons that *even of all the cattle* refers to camels. Look, Eleazar did not take a portion from them.⁴⁹ Perhaps they were few. Hence neither the number which they brought nor the amount which the Levites took is mentioned.⁵⁰

footnotes:
⁴⁸ Even all the cattle
⁴⁹ The camels.
⁵⁰ In our verse.



### Verse 32

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:32:1)


**יתר הבז.** חוץ מאשר אכלו על כן אמר יתר והזכיר מספרם כי היה מספר רב ולא שמענו שלל כמוהו גם כן הזכיר המכס כי גם הוא רב ומלת מכם מפועלי הכפל על משקל וממר ליולדתו והוא מגזרת תכסו על השה והטעם חלק:


OVER AND ABOVE THE BOOTY.⁵¹ Aside from what they consumed. Scripture therefore reads *yeter* (above).⁵² The Torah mentions the amount of spoil because it consisted of a vast number. We never heard of a similar amount of spoil.⁵³ Scripture also mentions the tribute,⁵⁴ for it too was very great. The word *mekhes* (tribute) (v. 37) comes from a double root.⁵⁵. It is vocalized like the word *memer* (bitterness)⁵⁶. in *and bitterness to her that bore him* (Prov. 17:25). *Mekhes* is related to the word *takhossu* (ye shall make your count) in *ye shall make your count for the lamb* (Ex. 12:4). The meaning of *mekhes* (tribute) is a portion.

footnotes:
⁵¹ Literally, the remnant of the booty.
⁵² Literally, the remnant of. See previous note.
⁵³ In Scripture.
⁵⁴ Given unto the Lord. See verses 37-41.
⁵⁵ Its root is kaf, samekh, samekh
⁵⁶ It is vocalized with two segols



### Verse 47

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:47:1)


ואמר **מן האדם ומן הבהמה.** שם כלל:


[BOTH OF MAN AND OF BEAST.] Scripture states *both of man and of beast*. Man⁵⁷. It takes in males and females, adults and children. and beast⁵⁸. It takes in cattle, asses, and camels. are general terms.

footnotes:
⁵⁷ Hebrew, adam
⁵⁸ Hebrew, behemah

THE OFFICERS⁵⁹.. I have already explained this term.⁶⁰

### Verse 49

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:49:1)


**אשר בידינו.** בעבור היותם ברשותם וכן ולקחת בידך עשרה אנשים:


THAT ARE UNDER OUR CHARGE. *Asher be-yadenu*⁶¹ means that they were in their⁶² charge. Compare, *yadekh* (in your hands) in *And take with thee ten loaves*⁶³ *in your hands*⁶⁴ (I Kings 14:3).⁶⁵

footnotes:
⁶¹ Literally, that are under our hand.
⁶² I.E. used the word their. However, it means our, as in the text.

### Verse 50

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:1)

AND WE HAVE BROUGHT. In thought or by mouth.⁶⁶ (and we brought) is a perfect. Hence its meaning must be, we dedicated.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:2)

ARMLETS. *Etzadah* (armlet) is an ornament placed on the arm. This is explicitly stated in Scripture.⁶⁷

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:3)


**וצמיד.** ליד:


AND BRACELETS. A *tzamid* (bracelet) is an ornament placed on the wrist.⁶⁸

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:4

SIGNET-RINGS. *Taba’at* (signet ring) refers to an ornament placed on the finger.⁶⁹

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:50:5)

EAR-RINGS. *Agil* is an earring. This is clearly stated in Scripture.⁷⁰ This ornament is possibly so called because it is round (*agul*).

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:50:6

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:51:1)

EVEN ALL THE WROUGHT JEWELS.⁷². I.E. renders this as articles fit for use. Scripture tells us that there was no broken object among them.
