### Verse 53

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 31:53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/31:53:1)


**אנשי הצבא בזזו איש לו.** הזכיר הכתוב זה להודיע כי זהב רב וכלי מעשה בזזו כל אחד:


FOR THE MEN OF WAR HAD TAKEN BOOTY, EVERY MAN FOR HIMSELF. Scripture mentions this to inform us that each one of the men of war took a vast amount of gold and wrought articles.



## Chapter 32


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:1:1)


**לבני ראובן.** כי הוא בן הגבירה:


THE CHILDREN OF REUBEN. For he was the son of the mistress.¹

footnotes:
¹ The son of Leah. Scripture mentions Reuben before Gad because he was the son of Leah whereas Gad was the son of Zilpah, who was a handmaiden. I.E. comments thus because the reverse is the case in verse 2.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:1:2)


**מקום מקנה.** כי שם מרעה טוב:


WAS A PLACE FOR CATTLE. For there it was good pasture.



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:2:1)


**ויבאו בני גד.** הם היו בעלי העצה בתחלה על כן הקדימם הכתוב:


THE CHILDREN OF GAD…CAME. They thought of this first. Hence Scripture places them first.²

footnotes:
² Before the tribe of Reuben, even though the tribe of Reuben was descended from Leah and the tribe of Gad from Zilpah,



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:5:1)


**ויאמרו יותן.** אמר ויאמרו פעם אחרת בעבור שארכו הדברים י"א כי את הוא פעול יותן ולא אדע למה זה כי הנה כתוב נעתם ארץ. יחלק הארץ ולא נשא אותם הארץ אשר הוא בא שמה בקרבו:


AND THEY SAID…LET THIS LAND BE GIVEN. Scripture repeats, *and they said* because they spoke at length.³). Our verse reads, *va-yomeru* (and they said). The latter is superfluous. Hence I.E.’s comment. Some say that the word *et* is the object of *yuttan* (be given).⁴ (let…[the] land be given). Now *eretz* is feminine. Thus our verse should read, *tuttan et ha-aretz*. Hence this opinion. I do not know why.⁵ Look, Scripture reads: *netam eretz*⁶ is masculine. We thus see that Scripture at times treats *eretz* as masculine. (the land is burnt up) (Is. 9:18); *yechalek ha-aretz*⁷ is masculine. (the land shall be divided) (Num. 26:55);⁸. I.E. omits the *et* in his quote. *ve-lo nasa otom ha-aretz*⁹ is masculine. (and the land was not able to bear them) (Gen. 13:6); and *asher hu va shammah be-kirbo*¹⁰ is masculine and refers to the land previously mentioned in the verse. See I.E. on Deut. 31:16. (whither they go to be among them) (Deut. 31:16).

footnotes:
³ Verse 2 tells us that the children of Gad and the children of Reuben spoke (va-yomeru
⁴ Our verse reads, yuttan et ha-aretz
⁵ These commentaries have to go to such extreme length.
⁶ Netem
⁷ Yechalek
⁸ The reading in Num. 26:55 is, yechalek et ha-aretz
⁹ Nasa
¹⁰ Be-kirbo



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:7:1)


**תניאון.** כמו תשברון:


YE TURN AWAY. *Teniyun* (ye turn away) means you break.



### Verse 9

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:9:1)


**ויעלו עד נחל אשכול.** כי העולים מצפון הלכו לנגב:


FOR WHEN THEY WENT UP UNTO THE VALLEY OF ESHCOL. For those who went up¹¹. According to I.E. this implies going north. to the north, went to the south.¹² to the north, for I.E. occasionally uses the *mem* to mean to. *Vat. Ebr*. 38 reads: They went from the south to the north.

footnotes:
¹¹ Scripture reads, and they went up
¹² They went north from Kadesh-barnea, where they were camped, to the south of Israel. See I.E. on Num. 13:17. Note that we have rendered mi-tzafon



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:11:1)


**לא מלאו.** קל הלמ"ד והוא מהבנין הכבד מלא קרנך שמן לא השלימו מה שהתחילו:


THEY HAVE NOT WHOLLY FOLLOWED ME. There is no *dagesh* in the *lamed* of *mile’u* (wholly followed). *Mile’u* is a *pi’el*.¹³ in it. Compare, *malle* (fill)¹⁴ is a *pi’el* and has a *dagesh* in it. See I.E.’s comments on verse 12. in *fill thy horn with oil* (I Sam. 16:1). They did not complete what they started.¹⁵ (because they have not wholly followed me).

footnotes:
¹³ And therefore should have a dagesh
¹⁴ Malle
¹⁵ The meaning of lo mile’u acharai



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:12:1)


**כלב בן יפנה.** בתחלה כאשר דבר השם ומלת מלאו כמו בקשו נפשי שלחו באש מקדשך:


CALEB THE SON OF JEPHUNNEH. Caleb is mentioned first, as in the case of God’s oath.¹⁶ The word *mile’u* (wholly followed) is similar to the word *bikshu* (have sought) in *have sought after my soul* (Ps. 44:5) and *shilchu* (they have set) in *They have set Thy sanctuary on fire* (Ps. 74:7).¹⁷ and *shilchu* are *pi’els* but, contrary to the rule, do not have a *dagesh* in them.

footnotes:
¹⁶ God mentioned Caleb before Joshua. See Num. 14:30. and I.E.’s comments thereto.
¹⁷ Both bikshu



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:13:1)


**וינעם.** מגזרת נע ונד בעבור שלא נחו:


AND HE MADE THEM WANDER. *Va-yeni’em* (and he made them wander) is related to the word *na* (a fugitive) in *a fugitive and a wanderer* (Gen. 4:12). Scripture reads *va-yeni’em* because they did not rest.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ During their sojourn in the wilderness.



### Verse 14

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:14:1)


**תרבות.** מגזרת אשר טפחתי ורביתי והוא קרוב מגזרת על כל רב ביתו והטעם גדול השנים:


A BROOD. The word *tarbut* (a brood) is connected to the word *ribbiti* (brought up) in *That I have dandled and brought up* (Lam. 2:22). It is close in meaning to the word *rav* (officers) in *all the officers* (rav) *of his house*¹⁹ (Esther 1:8). The meaning of *tarbut* is, grown up in years.

footnotes:
¹⁹ I.E. renders this as, all those brought up in his house.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:14:2)


**לספות.** כמו להוסיף רק הוא משרש אחר וכמוהו ספו על זבחיכם כי לא מצאנו בבעלי היו"ד מזה השרש בבנין הקל חסר יו"ד:


TO AUGMENT. *Li-sepot* (to augment) means the same as *le-hosif* (to add). However, it comes from a different root.²⁰ comes from the root *samekh, peh, heh*. *Hosif* comes from the root *yod, samekh, peh*. The word *sofu* (add) in *Add…unto your sacrifices* (Jer. 7:21) is similar,²¹; i.e., it comes from the root *samekh, peh, heh*. for we do not find this root²². among the stems that begin with a *yod* and drop the *yod* in the *kal*.²³.

footnotes:
²⁰ Li-sepot
²¹ To li-sepot
²² The root yod, samekh, heh
²³ Hence its root must be samekh, peh, heh



### Verse 16

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:16:1)


**וערים.** בתחלה לטפנו:


AND CITIES. First²⁴ for our little ones.

footnotes:
²⁴ Before we build the sheepfolds. This interpretations is contra the rabbis, who believed that the sons of Reuben and Gad valued their sheep over their little ones. See Rashi.



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:17:1)


**חשים.** על משקל מולים היו כמו מהירים מגזרת מהרה חושה אל תעמוד:


READY. The word *chushim* (ready) is vocalized like the word *mulim* (circumcised) in *were circumcised* (Josh. 5:5). It means quickly. It is related to the word *chushah* (hasten) in *Make speed, hasten, stay not* (I Sam. 20:38).



### Verse 19

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:19:1)


**והלאה.** נמצא על מקום ועל זמן והטעם ולמעלה:


AND FORWARD. The word *ve-hale’ah* (and forward) is used with regard to both place²⁵ (me-hale’ah) *Migdal-eder*. and time.²⁶ (ve-hale’ah). The meaning of *ve-hale’ah* is, and above.²⁷

footnotes:
²⁵ See Gen. 35:21: And Israel journeyed, and spread his tent beyond
²⁶ See Lev. 22:27. But from the eighth day and thenceforth
²⁷ That is, forward.



### Verse 21

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:21:1)


**עד הורישו.** השם:


UNTIL HE HATH DRIVEN OUT. God.²⁸ (He hath driven out) refers to God.

footnotes:
²⁸ Horisho



### Verse 22

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:22:1)


**לפני ה'.** כי שם הארון. לפני ה' כאשר פירשתי כי לעולם הוא לשבח:


BEFORE THE LORD. For the ark was there.²⁹ *Before the Lord* is to be taken as I have explained,³⁰ means before the divine glory which is upon the cherubim. for the phrase³¹. is always used in a positive sense.³² (before the Lord) literally means before the Lord’s face. This can be taken in a negative sense, that is, to anger God. See I.E. on the word *li-fene* in Gen. 11:28 (Vol. 1, p. 146).

footnotes:
²⁹ Among the people.
³⁰ See I.E. on Ex. 16:34 (Vol. 2, p. 332). Before the Lord
³¹ Li-fene Adonai
³² The word li-fene Adonai



### Verse 24

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:24:1)


אל"ף **לצאנכם.** נוסף כמו והאזניחו נהרות:


FOR YOUR SHEEP. The *alef* in *tzonakhem* (your sheep) is superfluous.³³. It is like the *alef* in *he’eznichu* (become foul) in *and the rivers shall become foul* (Is. 19:6).³⁴.

footnotes:
³³ The usual form of the word is tzonkhem
³⁴ Which is superfluous. The usual form is hiznichu



### Verse 27

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:27:1)


**כל חלוץ צבא.** כמו הארון הברית והטעם כל חלוץ חלוץ צבא:


EVERY MAN THAT IS ARMED FOR WAR. *Kol chalutz tzava* (every man that is armed for war) is similar to *ha-aron ha-berit*³⁵ has a *heh*, the definite article, prefixed to it. Such a word cannot be in the construct. Hence our phrase reads, the ark, the covenant. This should be read as if written, the ark, ark of the covenant. See I.E. on Gen. 2:9 (Vol. 1, p. 54, and the notes thereto). (the ark of the covenant) (Josh. 3:14). Its meaning is, *every man armed, armed for war*.³⁶ was apparently vocalized with a *kamatz*. This being the case, the verse read, armed, war. Hence I.E. suggests that our verse be read as if written, armed, armed for war (Motot). It should be noted that in our versions of Scripture the word *chalutz* is vocalized with a *chataf pattach* and is in the construct with *tzava*. It thus reads, armed for war.

footnotes:
³⁵ Ha-aron
³⁶ In I.E.’s edition of the Pentateuch the word chalutz



### Verse 29

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:29:1)


**ונתתם.** תתנו להם והוי"ו כפ"א רפה בל' ישמעאל כי כן משפט הלשון:


THEN YE SHALL GIVE THEM. The meaning of *u-netattem la-hem* is, you shall give them.³⁷ prefixed to *netattem* does not mean and. It is placed there for emphasis and is not to be translated. The *vav* is like the Arabic *fa*,³⁸ for such is the rule of the language.³⁹.

footnotes:
³⁷ In other words, the vav
³⁸ See I.E. On Gen. 1:2, (Vol. 1, p. 30).
³⁹ To employ a non-translatable yod



### Verse 30

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:30:1)


**ונאחזו בתככם.** חלק להם כבוד כי הטעם אם לא יעברו חלוצים תוליכום עמהם בעל כרחם ונאחזו או אם יתנחמו ונאחזו בתוככם ובאת זו המלה כמשפט בעלי היו"ד כי אותיות אהו"י מתחלפים:


THEY SHALL HAVE POSSESSIONS AMONG YOU. He gave them respect,⁴⁰ for the meaning of our verse is, if they⁴¹ will not go armed before you, then you shall force them to go with you and they shall have possessions among you.⁴² Or, if they change their minds, then they shall have possessions among you. The word *ve-nochazu* (they shall have possessions) follows the rules of a root that has a *yod* as its first letter,⁴³ is vocalized as if it came from the root *yod, chet zayin*, when in reality its root is *alef, chet, zayin*. for the letters *alef, heh, vav*, and *yod* interchange.

footnotes:
⁴⁰ He spoke to them with respect.
⁴¹ The tribes of Reuben and Gad and the half tribe of Manasseh.
⁴² Our verse says this in a diplomatic way.
⁴³ Ve-nochazu



### Verse 31

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:31:1)


**ויענו בני גד.** שנית חזוק:


AND THE CHILDREN OF GAD…ANSWERED. A second time for emphasis.



### Verse 32

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:32:1)


**נחנו.** הוא העיקר ואל"ף אנחנו נוסף ולא הזכיר עד עתה חצי שבט מנשה בעבור היותו חצי השבט:


WE WILL PASS. The form *nachnu* (we) is the original form of the word. The *alef* in *anachnu* (we)⁴⁴ is added. The half tribe of Manasseh is not mentioned until now because it was but half a tribe.

footnotes:
⁴⁴ The more usual employed term for we.



### Verse 38

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:38:1)


**ואת נבו.** יתכן שהוא הר נבו ששם נקבר משה כאשר אפרש:


AND NEBO. It is likely that the reference is to Mount Nebo, where, as I shall explain,⁴⁵ Moses was buried.

footnotes:
⁴⁵ See I.E. on Deut. 34:6.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:38:2)


**מוסבות.** אלה הערים הוסבו שמותם וכן שבמה:


THEIR NAMES BEING CHANGED. These cities had their names changed.⁴⁶ (their names being changed) is not the name of a city. The same applies to Sibmah.⁴⁷

footnotes:
⁴⁶ In other words, musabbot shem
⁴⁷ Its name was also changed.



### Verse 39

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:39:1)


**ויורש.** יש אומרים כי מכיר חי היה ולפי דעתי שמלת ויורש שבה אל הגבור שהיה במשפחה:


AND DISPOSSESSED. Some say that Machir was alive.⁴⁸ (and [he] dispossessed) applies. These commentaries interpret thus because verse 40 states, *And Moses gave Gilead unto Machir*. The latter is taken literally. However, I believe that *and dispossessed*⁴⁹ refers to the warrior that was in the family.⁵⁰

footnotes:
⁴⁸ That Machir the son of Manasseh was still alive, and that it is he to whom va-yoresh
⁴⁹ Literally, and he dispossessed.
⁵⁰ One of Machir’s descendants was a great warrior. He dispossessed the Amorites.



### Verse 40

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:40:1)


וטעם **למכיר בן מנשה.** לבני מכיר כמו ויאמר יהודה לשמעון אחיו:


[UNTO MACHIR THE SON OF MANASSEH.] The meaning of *unto Machir the son of Manasseh* is, unto the sons of Machir the son of Manasseh.⁵¹ Compare, *And Judah said unto Simeon his brother* (Jud. 1:3).⁵²

footnotes:
⁵¹ For Machir the son of Manasseh was no longer alive.
⁵² The meaning of which is, and the descendants of Judah said to the descendants of Simeon his brother, for the tribal patriarchs Reuben and Judah were no longer alive.



### Verse 41

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41:1)


**ויאיר בן מנשה.** הוא ממשפחת יהודה כי כן כתוב כי חצרון לקח בת מכיר בן מנשה והוליד ממנה שגוב ושגוב הוליד את יאיר שהיו לו הערים בערי הגלעד ונקרא על שם משפחת אמו גם יש כהנים שנקראו על שם בני ברזילי הגלעדי כי כן כתוב ואין פה מקום לשאול איך לקח יאיר נחלה עם שבט אחר כי ארץ כנען היא הנחלקת ולא ארץ האמורי שהיא מעבר לירדן מזרחה כי יש אמורי בארץ כנען והוא היה התקיף מכל שבעה גוים:


AND JAIR THE SON OF MANASSEH. Jair was from the family of Judah.⁵³ This is explicitly stated.⁵⁴ For Hezron⁵⁵ took the daughter of Machir the son of Manasseh, and she bore him Segub. Segub begot Jair,⁵⁶ who had cities among the cities of Gilead.⁵⁷. Jair is called after his mother’s family. There were also *kohanim* who were called after the name of the children of Barzillai the Gileadite.⁵⁸. The latter is clearly stated in Scripture.⁵⁹ This is no reason to ask: How did Jair⁶⁰ inherit among another tribe,⁶¹ for it was the land of Canaan that was divided,⁶² not the land of the Amorites that was on the eastern side of the Jordan,⁶³ for⁶⁴ there were Amorites in the land of Canaan⁶⁵ and they were the mightiest of the seven nations.

footnotes:
⁵³ From the tribe of Judah. This raises the question: Why does Scripture identify him as a son of Manasseh?
⁵⁴ In Scripture.
⁵⁵ The son of Judah.
⁵⁶ See I Chron. 2:1-22.
⁵⁷ Ibid
⁵⁸ The children of Barzillai were not kohanim
⁵⁹ See Ezra 2:61.
⁶⁰ Who was from the tribe of Judah.
⁶¹ Manasseh. Each tribe was allocated a portion by lot. Only members of the tribe received a portion in the land belonging to the tribe. The question thus arises, by what right did Jair receive a part of the land within the area allocated to Manasseh.
⁶² By lot.
⁶³ The land on the eastern bank of the Jordan was not allocated by lot to the tribe of Manasseh. Hence it was possible for Jair to obtain a part of the eastern bank of the Jordan by conquest.
⁶⁴ What follows is merely noted for informational purposes. It does no affect the interpretation.
⁶⁵ As there were in Transjordan.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 32:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41:2)


**חותיהם.** פירושו לפי מקומו כמו קריות:


THE VILLAGES THEREOF. *Chavvotehem* (villages thereof) is to be interpreted in context.⁶⁶ It means walled cities.

footnotes:
⁶⁶ The word is not found elsewhere in Scripture.



## Chapter 33


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:1:1)


**אלה מסעי.** כאשר חנו בני ישראל בערבות מואב וישבו שם חדשים עד שבנו הערים הנזכרים ולא זזו משם כי אם אחרי מות אהרן כתב משה כל המסעים:


THESE ARE THE STAGES. Moses recorded all the journeys of the Israelites when the children of Israel camped in the plains of Moab¹ and stayed there for a number of months, until the afore-mentioned cities² were built. They did not move from there until Aaron died.³

footnotes:
¹ Verse 48.
² The cities mentioned in Num. 32:33-42.
³ This appears to be an error, for Aaron died at Mount Hor before Israel came to the plains of Moab. See Num. 20:22-29. The proper reading should be, until Moses died.



### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2:1)


**את מוצאיהם.** איך יצאו ממקום למקום על כן אחריו למסעיהם:


THEIR GOINGS FORTH. How they went from place to place. Therefore *their goings forth* follows.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2:2)


**על פי ה'.** דבק עם למסעיהם:


BY THE COMMANDMENT OF THE LORD. This is connected to *stage by stage*.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Our text should be read: Stage by stage by the commandment of the Lord.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:3:1)


**ממחרת הפסח.** פירשתיו וכן ביד רמה:


ON THE MORROW AFTER THE PASSOVER. I have explained this,⁵ and *with a high hand* (Ex. 14:8)⁶ as well.

⁵ See I.E. on Lev. 23:11.
⁶ See I.E. on Lev. 23:11.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:4:1)


**ובאלהיהם עשה ה' שפטים.** כמו ראש דגון כי כן אמר השם למשה:


UPON THEIR GODS ALSO THE LORD EXECUTED JUDGMENTS. As in the incident with Dagan’s head.⁷ For God said so to Moses.⁸

⁷ I Sam. 5:4. Dagan fell headless, armless, and footless before the ark of God.

### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:7:1)


**וישב.** רמז לענן או לישראל:


AND TURNED BACK. This alludes to the cloud⁹ (and they journeyed) is in the plural. *Va-yashav* (and turned back) is in the singular. Thus our verse may be rendered: and they journeyed and it turned back. Hence I.E.’s interpretation. or to Israel.¹⁰

footnotes:
⁹ Which accompanied Israel. Va-yisu
¹⁰ According to this interpretation Scripture refers to Israel in the singular.



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:8:1)

FROM PENE-HAHIROTH. Pene-hahirot and Pi-hahiroth¹¹ refers to the same place. The mouth is in the face.¹² means mouth of; *pene* face of. Hence the names Pi-hahiroth and Pene-hahiroth for the same place.

¹² Pi

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:46:1)

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:49:1)

FROM BETH-JESHIMOTH. This is the valley which looks down upon the wasteland.²⁰ God then commanded Moses to warn Israel²¹ because they were soon to pass over.²²

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52:1)

THEIR FIGURED STONES. *Maskiyyotam* (their figured stones) is similar to *ve-even maskit* (any figured stone) (Lev. 26:1).


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:52:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52:2)

### Verse 54

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:54:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54:1)


**והתנחלתם.** כמו והתאויתם והפעול הארץ:


AND YE SHALL INHERIT THE LAND. *Ve-hitnachaltem* (and ye shall inherit) is similar to *ve-hitavvitem* (and ye shall mark out) (Num. 34 :10).²³ and are transitive. Most verbs in the *hitpa’el* are intransitive. Hence I.E.’s comment. *Ha-aretz* (the land) is the object.²⁴ is transitive.

footnotes:
²³ Both verbs are in the hitpa’el
²⁴ In other words, ve-hitnachaltem


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:54:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54:2)


וטעם להזכיר **לרב תרבו.** לדבק אחריו ואם לא תורישו:


Scripture mentions *to the more ye shall give the more inheritance* to connect *But if ye will not drive out the inhabitants* (v. 55) which comes after it.²⁵

footnotes:
²⁵ In other words, the law commanding Israel to drive out the Canaanites follows the law telling Israel to inherit Canaan, in order to warn Israel that they will not inherit the land if they permit Canaanites to dwell therein.



### Verse 55

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55:1)


**לשכים.** קוצים חדים מגזרת הסר מסוכתו. הנני סך את דרכך בסירים:


AS THORNS. *Sikkim* refers to sharp thorns. It is related to the word *mesukkato* (hedge thereof)²⁶ in *the hedge thereof* (Is. 5:5) and the word *sakh* (hedge) in *behold, I will hedge up thy way with thorns* (Hos. 2:8).

footnotes:
²⁶ The hedge spoken of in Is. 5:5 was made of sharp thorns.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 33:55:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55:2)


**ולצנינם.** פירושו לפי מקומו כמו ולשוטט בצדיכם שהם מכות בשוט או קוצים חדים כמו צנים פחים והנה עוד פי' להם גבול נחלתם שהם חייבים להוריש משם יושב הארץ:


AND AS PRICKS. The word *tzeninim* (pricks) is to be explained according to its context. It means the same as *u-leshotet be-tziddekhem* (a scourge in your sides) (Josh. 23:13), which²⁷ refers to a blow delivered by a whip.²⁸. Thus *ve-litzninim be-tziddekhem* (and as pricks in your sides) means, and lashes in their sides. On the other hand, the word *tzeninim* (pricks) might refer to sharp thorns, as in *Thorns* (tzinnim) *and snares in the way of the froward* (Prov. 22:5).²⁹ (and for pricks in your sides) means, and for thorns in their sides. Observe, Scripture further explains³⁰ the borders of their inheritance from which they are obligated to expel the inhabitants of the land.

footnotes:
²⁷ According to its context. See Weiser.
²⁸ So too the word tzeninim
²⁹ In this case, ve-litzninim be-tziddekhem
³⁰ In chapter 34.



## Chapter 34


### Verse 2

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:2:1)


**אל הארץ כנען.** כמו והנבואה עודד הנביא הנבואה נבואת עודד הנביא והטעם **אל הארץ.** ארץ כנען:


INTO THE LAND OF CANAAN. *Ha-aretz kena’an* (the land of Canaan) is similar to *ve-hanevu’ah oded ha-navi* (even the prophecy of Oded the prophet) (II Chron. 15:8), the meaning of which is, the prophecy, prophecy of Oded the prophet. The meaning of our clause is, the land, land of Canaan.¹ has the definite article prefixed to it. According to the rules of Hebrew grammar, a word with the definite article cannot be in the construct. The literal meaning of *ha-aretz kena’an* (the land of Canaan) thus is, the land Canaan. Hence I.E.’s comment that our verse be read as if written, *ha-aretz, eretz kena’an. Ve-hanevu’ah oded ha-navi* (even the prophecy of Oded the prophet) (II Chron. 15:8) is, for similar reasons, to be read, *ve-hanevu’ah nevu’at oded ha-navi*. See I.E. on Gen. 2:9 (Vol. 1, p. 54).

footnotes:
¹ Ha-aretz



### Verse 3

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:3:1)


**על ידי אדום.** מקום כמו על יד הירדן:


BY THE SIDE OF EDOM. The word *yede*² (side of) means place of. Compare, *by the side of* (yad)³ *the Jordan* (Num. 13:29).

footnotes:
² Literally, hands of. Hence I.E.’s comment.
³ Literally, hand of. See I.E. on Gen. 1:26 (Vol. 1, pp. 45-46).



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:5:1)


**נחלה מצרים.** אל נחל מצרים ואיננו היאור:


UNTO THE BROOK OF EGYPT. *Nachlah mitzrayim* means unto the brook of Egypt.⁴ suffixed to *nachlah* is in place of the preposition *el* (unto). It does not refer to the Nile.

footnotes:
⁴ For the heh



### Verse 6

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:6:1)


**הים הגדול.** הספרדי והטעם גבול מערב נחלתכם והיה הים הגדול והוא הגבול בעצמו:


THE GREAT SEA. The Spanish sea.⁵ The meaning of our verse is: And the Great Sea shall be your western border.⁶. This should be rendered, and for the western border not, and the sea. The word *yam* can mean either the sea or the west. Hence I.E.’s comment. The Great Sea itself shall serve as the border.⁷

footnotes:
⁵ I.E.’s term for the Mediterranean Sea.
⁶ Our verse reads, u-gavel yam
⁷ Our verse literally reads, and the border on the west, ye shall have the Great Sea, and a border. I.E. renders this, you shall have the Great Sea itself serve as your border on the west



### Verse 10

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10:1)


ואל"ף **והתאויתם.** תחת וי"ו והתוית תיו ואם הם ב' בנינים:


AND YE SHALL MARK OUT. The *alef* in *ve-hitavvitem* (and ye shall mark out) is in place of a *vav*.⁸. 38 reads, in place of a *tav*. The root of *ve-hitavvitem* appears to be *alef, vav, heh*. This root means to desire. *And you shall desire* appears out of place here. Hence I.E.’s suggestion that *ve-hitavvitem* comes from the root *tav, vav, heh*, which means to mark out. *And you shall mark out* fits in well within the context of our verse. Compare, *ve-hitvita*⁹. *tav* (and set a mark) (Ezek. 9:4). This is so, even though the forms differ.¹⁰ is in the *hitpa’el* while *ve-hitvita* is a *hif’il*.

footnotes:
⁸ Vat. Ebr
⁹ Which comes from the root tav, vav, heh
¹⁰ Ve-hitavvitem


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10:2)


**והתאויתם.** כמו עד תאות גבעות עולם:


AND YE SHALL MARK OUT. *Ve-hitavvitem* (and ye shall mark out) is similar to the word *ta’avat* (utmost bound) in *Unto the utmost bound of the everlasting hills* (Gen. 49:26).¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ I.E. makes the same point in Genesis. See Vol. 1, p. 447.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:11:1)


**ומחה.** כאשר אמרו קדמונינו תבירתא מגזרת ימחאו כף:


AND SHALL STRIKE. The word *machah* (strike) is to be interpreted as the ancients did. It means it shall break.¹² It is related to the word *yimcha’u* (clap) in *Let the floods clap their hands* (Ps. 98:8).

footnotes:
¹² When the boundary line reaches the lake of Kinneret, it will create a broken (not a straight) border (Weiser).



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:15:1)


**שני המטות.** פעם שנית לבאר מקום נחלתם:


THE TWO TRIBES. Scripture mentions this a second time¹³ in order to explain the place of their inheritance.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹³ The first half of our verse repeats the previous verse.
¹⁴ The second part of our verse tells us where the two and a half tribes received their inheritance.



### Verse 17

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:17:1)


**ינחלו.** פעל יוצא לשנים פעולים וכן ונחלתנו על דעת המדקדקים:


SHALL TAKE POSSESSION. *Yinchalu* (shall take possession) is a causative.¹⁵. According to I. E. the word *yinchalu* here should be rendered, shall cause you to take possession, rather than shall take possession. The same applies to the word *u-nechaltanu* (and take us for Thine inheritance) (Ex. 34:9)¹⁶ (and take us for Thine inheritance) should be rendered, you will cause us to inherit. See I.E.’s short commentary on Ex. 34:9. in the opinion of the grammarians.

footnotes:
¹⁵ Even though it is a kal
¹⁶ According to I.E., u-nechaltanu


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:17:2)


**אלעזר.** ראשונה כי על פיו יצא יהושע גם הוא בן אהרן. ולא הזכיר נשיא לבני ראובן וגד כי כבר לקחו חלקם והמפרשים אמרו כי החל מיהודה כאשר יצא הגורל בתחלה רק יש טענה גדולה עליהם אף על פי שהפירוש נכון יהיה נראה לפי דעתינו:


ELEAZAR. He is mentioned first¹⁷ because Joshua went out by his command. Furthermore, Eleazar was also the son of Aaron. Scripture does not mention the princes of the tribes of Reuben or Gad,¹⁸ for they¹⁹ had already taken their share. The commentaries say that Scripture starts²⁰ with Judah,²¹ for that is the way the lot came out at first.²² However, there is a great argument against them.²³ Nevertheless, the comments appear correct to us.²⁴

footnotes:
¹⁷ Before Joshua.
¹⁸ In listing the princes of the tribes in verses 17-28.
¹⁹ Their tribes.
²⁰ In listing the tribes.
²¹ Even though Simeon was older.
²² When the land was actually divided. See Joshua 15:1.
²³ If Scripture followed the order in which the tribes actually received their inheritance, the children of Joseph should have followed Judah, for they took their inheritance after Judah. See Joshua 16.
²⁴ For no one can offer a better reason than these commentaries for listing Judah first (Weiser).



### Verse 28

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 34:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/34:28:1)


**פדהאל.** אם נעלם הה"א הם שתי מלות שוות בטעם כי הה"א איננה מהאותיות המשך:


PEDAHEL. If the *heh* in Pedahel is silent then we have two words each with its own meaning,²⁵ is silent only when it comes at the end of a word. It is not silent when it comes in the middle of a word. Since the *heh* in the name Pedahel is silent we must interpret it as two words, *padah el* (God redeemed). I.E.’s point is that while many of the Hebrew names containing God’s name are partially abbreviated, this is not the case with Pedahel. for the *heh* is not an implied letter.²⁶

footnotes:
²⁵ The heh
²⁶ In the middle of a word. See I.E. on Ex. 3:15 (Vol. 2, p. 85).

AND OPEN LAND. The meaning of *migrash* (open land) is known.¹

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:10:1)

INTO THE LAND OF CANAAN. The meaning of *artzah kena’an* (into the land of Canaan) is known.² of *artzah* is in place of the preposition *el*. Thus *artzah kena’an* means into the land of Canaan. This chapter, like others,³ is connected.⁴

³ Like other chapters which I.E. had previously pointed out.

### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:11:1)


**והקריתם.** מגזרת קריה:


THEN YE SHALL APPOINT YOU CITIES. The word *ve-hikritem* (then shall ye appoint you) is related to the word *kiryah* (cities).⁵, a wall. *Kiryah* is thus a walled city. *Ve-hikritem lakhem arim* thus means, and you shall build walled cities.

footnotes:
⁵ From the word kir



### Verse 15

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:15:1)


**לבני ישראל.** איננו דבק עם הפסוק הראשון כי בפסוק הראשון אמר כי אלה הערים תהיינה ערי מקלט ולא אמר מקלט למי אחר כן באר כי לבני ישראל ולגר הגר בתוכם תהיינה שש הערים האלה מקלט:


FOR THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL. This is not connected to the previous verse,⁶ for the preceding verse states that these cities will be cities of refuge. However, it does not state for whom they shall serve as cities of refuge. Scripture then explains⁷ that these six cities shall serve as cities of refuge *for the children of Israel, and for the stranger…who lives among them*.

footnotes:
⁶ Our verse is not a continuation of the previous verse. It is an independent clause.
⁷ In our verse.



### Verse 19

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:19:1)


**בפגעו בו.** בשבר הפ"ה כמו בשברי לכם כי שמות הפועלים ישתנו:


WHEN HE MEETETH HIM. The *feh* of *bi-figo* (when he meeteth him) is vocalized with a *chirik*⁸. Compare *shomoro, kotvo*, etc. like the word *shivri* (I break) in *when I break* (Lev. 26:26), for vocalization changes in the infinitive.⁹

footnotes:
⁸ In place of a short kamatz
⁹ Not all infinitives follow the same vocalization.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:20:1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:22:1

### Verse 25

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:25:1)


**עד מות.** יש אומר עד מות עד חפוש וכמוהו תמות חכמה ולא אמרו כלום כי עמכם תמות חכמה הוא כמשמעו וגם כן עד מות הכהן הגדול כי הוא יכפר בעד ישראל וזה המאורע אירע בימיו:


THEREIN UNTIL THE DEATH OF THE HIGH PRIEST. Some say that the meaning of *until the death of* is, until the investigation of.¹³ They compare it to *And wisdom shall die with you* (Job. 12:2).¹⁴ However, what they say is worthless. For *And wisdom shall die with you* means exactly what it says,¹⁵ means shall die, not shall search out. as does *until the death of the high priest*.¹⁶ For the high priest atones on behalf of Israel, and this incident happened during his lifetime.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹³ Until the high priest investigates the death of the victim. For other interpretations see Filwarg.
¹⁴ They render this, and with you is wisdom searched out.
¹⁵ Tamut
¹⁶ Which is to be taken literally.
¹⁷ Hence the murderer shall remain in the city of exile to remind the high priest of his failing. The high priest’s piety should have prevented the murder (see Rashi). Or, the murderer shall remain in exile to remind the high priest to pray constantly that God forgive Israel for the bloodshed in its midst.

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:26:1

**את גבול.** מן גבול כמו כצאתי את העיר:


THE BORDER OF HIS CITY. *Et gevul* means beyond¹⁸ the border.¹⁹ is usually not translated. Hence I.E. points out that here it means from. Compare, *et ha-ir* (out of the city) in *as I am gone out of the city* (Ex. 9:29).

¹⁹ The word et

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:27:1

BLOODGUILTINESS. I have already explained the meaning of *dam* (bloodguiltiness).²⁰

### Verse 33

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33:1)

SO YE SHALL NOT POLLUTE. Do evil in secret.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33:2)


**שופכו.** פועל כמו ובא גואלו:


SHED IT. *Shofkho* (shed it) is a verb.²¹ (shedder) and the word *shelo* (its) Compare *go’alo* (his redeemer)²² (redeems) and *shelo* (him) (translated according to I.E.). in *then shall his redeemer…come* (Lev. 25:25).

### Verse 34

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 35:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/35:34:1)


**ולא תטמא.** החנופה את הארץ או הוא ציווי בעבור כי אני שוכן בתוכם ולא לכבוד הארץ רק לכבוד בני ישראל על כן אמר שוכן בתוך בני ישראל:


AND THOU SHALT NOT DEFILE²³. *Titamme* can be rendered either as you shall defile or it shall defile. Hence the interpretations which follow. THE LAND. The pollution shall not defile the land.²⁴, it shall not defile. Or it is a command.²⁵, you shall not defile. The land shall not be polluted because I dwell among them.²⁶. I do not dwell there out of respect for the land but out of respect for the children of Israel. Therefore Scripture says, *I the Lord dwell in the midst of the children of Israel* (v. 34).

²³ Hebrew, titamme
²⁴ This interpretation renders ve-lo titamme
²⁵ This interpretation renders ve-lo titamme
²⁶ I.E.’s paraphrase of the Lord dwells in the midst of the children of Israel



## Chapter 36


### Verse 1

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1:1)


**ויקרבו.** גם זאת הפרשה דבקה עם כאשר צוה משה לתת מנחלת ישראל ערים ללוים:


CAME NEAR. This chapter¹ also is connected to the one that comes before it. When Moses commanded the Israelites to give the Levites cities from the inheritance of Israel [the heads of the fathers’ houses drew near].

footnotes:
¹ Like other chapters.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1:2)


**ראשי אבות.** הם הנשיאים:


THE HEADS OF THE FATHERS’ HOUSES. The reference is to the princes.² (v. 1).

footnotes:
² Of the tribes. Hence Scripture reads, before the princes, the heads of the fathers’ houses of the children of Israel



### Verse 4

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:4:1)


**ואם יהיה היובל.** ואפילו שיהיה היובל לא נקוה לשוב הנחלה אלינו רק תהיה תמיד נוספת על נחלת המטה:


AND WHEN THE JUBILEE…SHALL BE. Even if the jubilee shall be,³ (and when the jubilee shall be), and even if the jubilee shall be. we have no hope of the inheritance returning to us, for it shall be added to the inheritance of the tribe.⁴

footnotes:
³ I.E. renders ve-im yiheyah ha-yovel
⁴ Into which they married.



### Verse 5

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:5:1)


**כן מטה בני יוסף.** כי ראשי האבות דברו בעבור כל המטה:


THE TRIBE OF THE SONS OF JOSEPH SPEAKETH RIGHT. For the heads of the fathers’ houses spoke on behalf of the entire tribe.



### Verse 7

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7:1)


וטעם **תסב.** שפעם תסוב מפה ומפה (ס"א תסוב מכה וכה) בין הם בין אחרים:


[SO SHALL NO INHERITANCE OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL REMOVE FROM TRIBE TO TRIBE.] The meaning of *tissov* (remove) is, shall not turn⁵ comes from the root *samekh, bet, bet*, meaning to turn. from here to there,⁶ (from here and from here) to *mi-po le-po* (from here to there) (Filwarg). be it from them⁷ or from others.⁸

footnotes:
⁵ Shall not move around. Tissov
⁶ Emending mi-po u-mi-po
⁷ The tribe of Joseph, who argued their case before Moses.
⁸ Any other tribe.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7:2)


וטעם **לא תסוב נחלה.** מהיום קודם נחלת הארץ:


The meaning of *so shall no inheritance be removed* is, no inheritance shall be removed from this day and onward even though they had not as yet inherited the land.



### Verse 8

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:8:1)


**וכל בת יורשת נחלה.** אחר ניחול הארץ על כן אמר הכתוב ולא תסוב נחלה פעם שנית:


AND EVERY DAUGHTER, THAT POSSESSETH AN INHERITANCE. After the inheritance of the land. Scripture therefore states a second time, *So shall no inheritance remove* [*from one tribe to another tribe*] (v. 9).⁹

footnotes:
⁹ In other words, verse 7 speaks of the present. Verse 8 speaks of the future.



### Verse 11

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:11:1)


**מחלה תרצה.** הזכירם הכתוב בתחלה כאשר נולדו ועתה הזכיר' כאשר נשאו לאנשים בתחלה:


MAHLAH, TIRZAH. Scripture first mentioned them¹⁰ in the order of their birth. It now mentions them in the order in which they were first taken by men in marriage.

footnotes:
¹⁰ In Num. 27:1.



### Verse 12

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:12:1)


**ממשפחות.** לאות כי בני דודיהן לא היו כולם אחים:


THEY WERE MARRIED INTO THE FAMILIES. This¹¹ is a sign that their cousins¹² were not all brothers.¹³

footnotes:
¹¹ The word families.
¹² The cousins who married the daughters of Zelophehad.
¹³ If they were, then Scripture would have read, they were married into the “family” rather than they married into the “families.”



### Verse 13

###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13:1)


**אלה המצות.** של הנחלה והנדר וכל בת יורשת נחלה ומשפטי הדם:


THESE ARE THE COMMANDMENTS. Relating to inheritance,¹⁴ vows,¹⁵ and every daughter that possesseth an inheritance,¹⁶ and the laws regarding bloodshed.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁴ Num. 33:50-56; 34; 35:1-8.
¹⁵ Num. 30:2-17.
¹⁶ Num. 36:8,9.
¹⁷ Num. 35:9-34.


###### Ibn Ezra on Numbers 36:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ibn%20Ezra%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13:2)


**על ירדן ירחו** מזרחה:


BY THE JORDAN AT JERICHO. On the eastern bank.
